  Anton把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Anton病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004446_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.ask-neighbour-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/door-closed` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Weiss把门开了一条缝，说自家也不够烧。Hedwig提着空筐站了一会儿，听见门后有孩子在哭，便没有再敲。回屋后，她把椅子拆了一条腿，塞进炉子。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +refused_neighbour_help; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1934.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Hedwig嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Hedwig用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-emil-weiss; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1934.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Emil Weiss指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rehearsal-broken-up` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, vienna, urban, crisis
  排练到第二幕，房东下来敲门，说楼下的住户嫌吵，再排就涨租。Emil Kral把布景折起来，演员们抱着道具站在楼梯口。Hedwig最后走，关灯时看见舞台地板上有一圈粉笔画的走位线。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1935.05** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  Hedwig买了鱼，回家路上看见Anton蹲在门口刮空锅。Hedwig把鱼分成两半，一半给了Anton。Anton接过去时手在围裙上擦了又擦，第二天还回来一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1935.06** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-money` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Sigmund Freud把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Sigmund Freud把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; flag +refused_curse_work; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.07** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/lie-that-stuck` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton告诉所有人Hedwig偷了东西。没有人找到赃物，但每个人都在传。Hedwig去市场时，摊主把零钱放在柜台上，不再递到手里。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.1; flag +false_accusation; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:90_

- **1935.07** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, betrayal, family
  Hedwig走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004776_

- **1935.07** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Anton第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004776_

- **1936.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fear-spreads` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Anton病倒以后，楼里开始有人避开Hedwig。没有人说是诅咒，但每个人都在数Hedwig最近和谁说过话。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; flag +curse_suspected; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004776_

- **1936.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `r13-edu.accounting-course::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/debit-credit-reversed` tags: education, accounting, bookkeeping, work, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig把借方和贷方弄反，试算表怎么也不平。Hedwig熬到半夜，把整本账重抄一遍，第二天交给Anna Bauer时，还是错了一笔。Anna Bauer让Hedwig从第一页重新学起，Hedwig把错的那页折起来，没舍得撕。Anna Bauer把自己的旧账本借给Hedwig，让他从头再抄。这次没有错。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; skill_

- **1936.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Anton把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Hedwig把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Anton低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1936.06** `p9-work-printshop.wage-dispute/keep-own-ledger` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, wages, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从排字台边走过去了，皮鞋声停了一下又起，Hedwig没有抬头，只把本子往怀里按了按。
  _effects: cash -1 (拒签后被压减的计件活); skill_

- **1936.10** `r13-rel.friend-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::friend-loan-crisis/the-iou-went-unpaid` tags: relationship, friend, money, debt, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  借出去的钱到了年底也没有还。Hedwig在账本上把那一行圈起来，又划掉。Emil Weiss照样来串门，照样喝Hedwig家的茶。有一次Hedwig提起，Emil Weiss说下个月，下个月又下个月。
  _effects: cash -5 (借出去的那张钞票); flag +bad_loan; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1937.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:109; reputation -0.020000000000000018; condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1937.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anton追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; food_

- **1937.01** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.return-address` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor
  她照着铜牌一路问到那栋楼，门房说裁缝天不亮就出门了，天黑才回。Hedwig把狗绳在栏杆上绕了两圈，蹲下来拍了拍狗脑袋，狗伸出舌头舔了舔她的手背，才跟着门房往门廊里走。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_expire:public-scandal` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_tick, state:public-scandal
  Hedwig的「公开丑闻」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:90_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/split-good-wood` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig练到半夜，刨刃吃得太深，一块好料从中间裂开。木料钱要从下周的菜钱里出，Emil Weiss听见响声，隔着墙问是不是在劈柴。Hedwig把裂开的板子翻过来，看见木纹里有一道旧钉眼，才明白这块料本来就不该用。
  _effects: cash -2 (第四十个榫头); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +work13_split_good_wood; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1937.06** `r3-war.factory-accident::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/lifted-press-off-hand` tags: war, factory, accident, injury, speedup, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Kral的手被冲床压住，Hedwig用撬棍把滑块顶起来，指甲盖翻了两片。Katharina Lechner赶来时，Emil Kral已经疼得说不出话；Anna Winter说冲床没有护手，是“老规矩”。Hedwig把撬棍扔在地上，说“那你自己来试”。
  _effects: condition; flag +lifted_press_off_mate_hand; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the press has no hand guard”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1938.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Emil Weiss请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Emil Weiss把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 30 -> 40; reputation -0.03999999999999998; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.030000000000000138_

- **1938.01** `r13-work.report-defect::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/form-lost` tags: work, safety, bureaucracy, factory, whistleblowing, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig把报修单交上去，三天没有回音。再去问，Emil Kral说单子夹在工资册里弄丢了，让Hedwig重写一张。那台机器还在转，螺栓一天比一天松；Hedwig每次经过都绕开半步，耳朵却一直听着它的声音。
  _effects: condition; flag +work13_safety_form_lost; knowledge “the repair request never reached the maintenance book”_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Hedwig在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Rosa Weiss记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Hedwig带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  Hedwig想起这家上次的诊费也没结，把处方推回去，只留下那张饮食单，说先照这个吃，药缓缓。Emil Weiss把硬币收回口袋，站在药房门口没走，最后把饮食单折成小方块捏在手心，回去的路上下起了雨。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1939.11** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.refused` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Hedwig在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的走廊里拦住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，话还没说完，对方就摆手说账上没这笔规矩。Hedwig回车间时，工头正往布告栏上钉新的计件单。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.050000000000000044_

- **1940.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Emil Weiss把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Emil Weiss在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:135; reputation -0.06000000000000005; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; flag +denounced_by_known_person_

- **1940.01** `kernel-migrate.return-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/civic-return-home` tags: migration, return, border, family, war, vienna, urban, crisis
  火车到边境时天刚亮，Rosa Weiss把她的证件翻了三遍，问她在外面做什么。她说“做工”，对方在表格上写“经济移民”；她纠正说“回家”，对方没有改。她把表格上的“经济移民”看了很久，最后没有要求改。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; residence; cash -6 (回程票、过境费和行李托运费); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000164:child; flag +returned_home; knowledge “边境表格会把返乡写成经济移民，改不改由官员决定”_

  Hedwig没有开门。Anton在门外站了一夜，第二天去了救济站；Hedwig从窗户里看见他们排队，把窗帘拉上了。三个月后，Anton在市场上遇见Hedwig，点了个头；Hedwig回家，把厨房里多出来的那张草垫卷起来，塞进了柜子顶。
  _effects: flag +refused_to_merge_households; role; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1940.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Hedwig看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1941.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/it-turns-in-the-hand` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Hedwig收了钱，却在灵堂里试着把那个名字指向Gustav Moser。蜡烛刚点着，Hedwig自己的手先抖起来，火苗顺着袖口爬上去。Gustav Moser扑灭了火，没有喊人，只把门打开。Hedwig走出去时，听见身后有人在数剩下的蜡烛。
  _effects: reputation -0.07000000000000006; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000928:child; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.6 -> 0.7; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:142; flag +curse_recoiled_on_holder_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.seek-friend::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::seek-friend-war/raid-friend` tags: task, social, friendship, loneliness, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Hofer在防空洞口分给Hedwig半支烟，两人背靠潮湿的砖墙，听远处炮声。Hedwig说自己怕的不是炮，是回家后没人说话；Helene Hofer把烟头按灭，说那就每天这个时辰在这儿等。
  _effects: condition; flag +air_raid_friend; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; knowledge “the cellar entrance is quiet after the all-clear”_

- **1941.04** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  苹果堆里混着几个已经发软的，摊主用围裙兜着往她篮子里倒，说拿回去喂猪也不亏。Hedwig在井边洗了半篮子，削皮时看见果核边有一圈褐色的痕，刀子挖下去，甜味还是冒出来。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1941.06** `r3-crime.press-coverage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::two-sheet-paper/story-spiked` tags: crime, press, court, reputation, truth, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig把稿子里的名字换成了“某男子”，Anna Winter把版面撤下来，说“没有名字谁看”。Hedwig坚持不改，第二天，桌子被清空，抽屉里的采访本被扔在走廊。Emil Kraus后来托人送来一包烟，Hedwig没有收。
  _effects: flag +story_spiked_to_protect_suspect; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the editor wanted a name before the verdict”; unemployment (dismissed for refusing to name a suspect before trial); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:155; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Hedwig 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Gustav Moser 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000928:child_

- **1942.07** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  Hedwig把腌鲱鱼放在黑面包上，咸味够配一大杯水。Luise Kral拿着盐罐过来，看见鱼就说拿卷心菜换。换完以后，两家灶上都多了一样东西。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1942.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把通知念给Emil Winter听，Emil Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hedwig去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food_

- **1943.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-money` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Emil Winter把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Emil Winter把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000272:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 25 -> 17; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cannot-read-lessons` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig读到第三课就跟不上，题目里的“比例”和“折旧”都认不全。Hedwig把讲义压在箱底，直到邮差来问续费，才发现已经欠了三期。钱退不回来，Hedwig把没拆的信封当柴烧了。第二年，Hedwig在厂里听人念夜校的招生简章，才知道那门课还在办。
  _effects: cash -3 (邮包里的第七课); mood -0.06000000000000005_

  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  Maria Stein在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Maria Stein也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。

  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); condition; flag +waited_for_foster_placement; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006902:child_

  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Berta 5；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Anton Hofer 5把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:208_

- **1948.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Anton Hofer 5在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Anton Hofer 5也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:209_

- **1948.01** `p9-work-career.client-list/p9-work-career.client-list.uptown` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, clients, reputation
  医生的太太要人上门修一只核桃木衣柜，价钱开得高，但要下午三点到，活做完还要把刨花扫净带走。Hedwig犹豫后应下，穿上唯一一件没沾漆的外衣去了。活干得顺利，主顾多加了一克朗。可回作坊时，老主顾在门口等他，说现在要请动他得先看时辰了。
  _effects: cash 5 (上门修衣柜的工钱与小费); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  Anton Hofer 5趁Hedwig不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Hedwig回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Anton Hofer 5站在门里，说房东已经同意。

  Anton Hofer 5在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Anton Hofer 5。

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Anton Hofer 5病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。

  夜班交接后，Hedwig从工具柜里摸出那只旧齿轮，换回去，又把新齿轮塞进棉纱袋。第二天产量掉了，Anna Winter围着织机转了三圈，骂谁动了手脚。Hedwig没有作声，只把梭子重新穿好；Helene Weiss 2在旁边看着，嘴角动了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +swapped_back_the_speedup_gear; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the old gear is hidden in a cotton sack”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1949.01** `statistical_death/accident` tags: death
  Hedwig在Lechner-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt附近出了事故。工友和路人把人抬到门口，等医生赶到时已经没有了呼吸。Anton Hofer 5在场。
  _effects: death (意外事故)_
